attribute vec4 a_vertex;
attribute vec4 a_color;
attribute vec2 a_texcoord0;
uniform mat4 u_mvpMatrix;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying highp vec2 TexCoord0;
void main (void)
gl_Position = u_mvpMatrix * a_vertex;
inColor = a_color;
TexCoord0 = a_texcoord0;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying highp vec2 TexCoord0;
uniform sampler2D texUnit0;
void main (void)
mediump vec4 color;
mediump vec3 rgb;
mediump float a;
= inColor.rgb;
= inColor.a * texture2D(texUnit0, TexCoord0).a;
color = vec4(rgb, a);
color = clamp(color, 0.0, 1.0);
gl_FragColor = color;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying highp vec2 TexCoord0;
uniform sampler2D texUnit0;
void main (void)
mediump vec4 color;
color = inColor;
color = color * texture2D(texUnit0, TexCoord0);
gl_FragColor = color;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying highp vec2 TexCoord0;
uniform sampler2D texUnit0;
void main (void)
mediump vec4 color;
mediump vec3 rgb;
mediump float a;
= inColor.rgb + texture2D(texUnit0, TexCoord0).rgb;
= inColor.a * texture2D(texUnit0, TexCoord0).a;
color = vec4(rgb, a);
color = clamp(color, 0.0, 1.0);
gl_FragColor = color;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying highp vec2 TexCoord0;
uniform sampler2D texUnit0;
void main (void)
mediump vec4 color;
mediump vec4 colorLow;
mediump float minValue;
mediump float valueUnit;
colorLow = texture2D(texUnit0, TexCoord0);
if (colorLow.r > 0.5)
valueUnit = (1.0 - colorLow.r) * 2.0;
minValue = colorLow.r - (1.0 - colorLow.r);
color.r = inColor.r * valueUnit + minValue;
else
valueUnit = colorLow.r * 2.0;
color.r = inColor.r * valueUnit;
if (colorLow.g > 0.5)
valueUnit = (1.0 - colorLow.g) * 2.0;
minValue = colorLow.r - (1.0 - colorLow.g);
color.g = inColor.g * valueUnit + minValue;
else
valueUnit = colorLow.g * 2.0;
color.g = inColor.g * valueUnit;
if (colorLow.b > 0.5)
valueUnit = (1.0 - colorLow.b) * 2.0;
minValue = colorLow.r - (1.0 - colorLow.b);
color.b = inColor.b * valueUnit + minValue;
else
valueUnit = colorLow.b * 2.0;
color.b = inColor.b * valueUnit;
color.a = colorLow.a;
color = clamp(color, 0.0, 1.0);
gl_FragColor = color;
attribute vec4 a_vertex;
attribute vec4 a_color;
attribute vec4 a_normal;
attribute vec2 a_texcoord0;
attribute vec2 a_texcoord1;
uniform mat4 u_mvpMatrix;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying mediump vec2 TexCoord0;
varying mediump vec2 TexCoord1;
void main (void)
mediump vec4 normal = a_normal;
gl_Position = u_mvpMatrix * a_vertex;
inColor = a_color;
TexCoord0 = a_texcoord0;
TexCoord1 = a_texcoord1;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying mediump vec2 TexCoord0;
varying mediump vec2 TexCoord1;
uniform sampler2D texUnit0;
uniform sampler2D texUnit1;
void main (void)
mediump vec4 color;
mediump vec3 rgb;
mediump float a;
= inColor.rgb * (texture2D(texUnit0, TexCoord0).rgb + texture2D(texUnit1, TexCoord1).rgb);
= inColor.a * (texture2D(texUnit0, TexCoord0).a + texture2D(texUnit1, TexCoord1).a);
color = vec4(rgb, a);
color = clamp(color, 0.0, 1.0);
gl_FragColor = color;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying mediump vec2 TexCoord0;
varying mediump vec2 TexCoord1;
uniform sampler2D texUnit0;
uniform sampler2D texUnit1;
void main (void)
mediump vec4 color;
color = inColor;
color = inColor * texture2D(texUnit0, TexCoord0) * texture2D(texUnit1, TexCoord1);
gl_FragColor = color;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying mediump vec2 TexCoord0;
varying mediump vec2 TexCoord1;
uniform sampler2D texUnit0;
uniform sampler2D texUnit1;
void main (void)
mediump vec4 color;
color = inColor;
color = inColor * texture2D(texUnit0, TexCoord0);
gl_FragColor = color;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying mediump vec2 TexCoord0;
varying mediump vec2 TexCoord1;
uniform sampler2D texUnit0;
uniform sampler2D texUnit1;
uniform mediump vec4 ambient;
void main (void)
mediump vec4 color;
mediump vec4 tex2Color = texture2D(texUnit1, TexCoord1);// * 2.0;
mediump vec4 tex1Color = texture2D(texUnit0, TexCoord0);// * 2.0;
color = inColor * (tex1Color * 0.9 + tex2Color * 0.1);// * 0.1;
gl_FragColor = color;
attribute vec4 a_vertex;
attribute vec4 a_color;
attribute vec2 a_texcoord0;
uniform mat4 u_mvpMatrix;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying mediump vec2 TexCoord0;
void main (void)
gl_Position = u_mvpMatrix * a_vertex;
inColor = a_color;
TexCoord0 = a_texcoord0;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying mediump vec2 TexCoord0;
uniform sampler2D texUnit0;
uniform mediump vec4 ambient;
void main (void)
mediump vec4 color;
mediump float x = TexCoord0.x;
mediump float y = TexCoord0.y;
color = inColor;
color = color * texture2D(texUnit0, vec2(x, y)).r + ambient * texture2D(texUnit0, vec2(x, y)).g;
gl_FragColor = color;
attribute vec4 a_vertex;
attribute vec4 a_color;
uniform mat4 u_mvpMatrix;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
void main (void)
gl_Position = u_mvpMatrix * a_vertex;
inColor = a_color;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
void main (void)
gl_FragColor = inColor;
attribute vec4 a_vertex;
attribute vec4 a_color;
attribute vec2 a_texcoord0;
attribute vec4 a_normal;
uniform mat4 u_mvpMatrix;
uniform mat4 u_modelMatrix;
uniform mat3 u_normalMatrix;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying mediump vec2 TexCoord0;
varying mediump vec3 normal;
varying mediump vec3 ecPosition3;
void main (void)
gl_Position = u_mvpMatrix * a_vertex;
inColor = a_color;
TexCoord0 = a_texcoord0;
mediump vec4 ecPosition = u_modelMatrix * a_vertex;
ecPosition3 = (vec3 (ecPosition)) / ecPosition.w;
normal = u_normalMatrix * vec3 (a_normal);
normal = normalize(normal);
uniform mediump vec4 ambient;
uniform mediump vec4 diffuse;
uniform mediump vec4 specular;
uniform mediump float shinness;
uniform mediump vec3 eyePosition;
uniform mediump vec3 lightPosition;
uniform mediump vec4 lambient;
uniform mediump vec4 ldiffuse;
uniform mediump vec4 lspecular;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying mediump vec2 TexCoord0;
varying mediump vec3 normal;
varying mediump vec3 ecPosition3;
uniform sampler2D texUnit0;
void main (void)
mediump vec4 color;
mediump float nDotVP;       
mediump float nDotHV;       
mediump float pf;           
mediump vec3  VP;           
mediump vec3  halfVector;   
color = inColor;
VP = lightPosition - ecPosition3;
VP = normalize(VP);
halfVector = normalize(VP + eyePosition);
nDotVP = max(0.0, dot(normal, VP));
nDotHV = max(0.0, dot(normal, halfVector));
if (nDotVP == 0.0)
pf = 0.0;
else
pf = pow(nDotHV, shinness);
color = lambient * ambient + ldiffuse * nDotVP * diffuse;
color += lspecular * pf * specular;
color = clamp( color, 0.0, 1.0 );
color = color * texture2D(texUnit0, TexCoord0);
color.a = 1.0;
gl_FragColor = color;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying highp vec2 TexCoord0;
uniform sampler2D texUnit0;
uniform mediump vec4 lambient;
void main (void)
mediump vec4 color;
mediump vec3 rgb;
mediump float a;
= inColor.rgb + texture2D(texUnit0, TexCoord0).rgb;
= inColor.a * texture2D(texUnit0, TexCoord0).a;
color = lambient * vec4(rgb, a);
color = clamp(color, 0.0, 1.0);
gl_FragColor = color;
varying mediump vec4 inColor;
varying highp vec2 TexCoord0;
uniform sampler2D texUnit0;
uniform mediump vec4 lambient;
void main (void)
mediump vec4 color;
color = inColor;
color = lambient * color * texture2D(texUnit0, TexCoord0);
gl_FragColor = color;
